# 专升本英语试题（单选）

> 共2774题。

---

## 第一部分 词汇

### UNIT 1

**1、** The good chance the child had looked forward to _______ at last.
- A. coming
- B. comes
- C. come
- D. came
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**2、** I really don’t know _______ I kept the secret papers.
- A. where was it
- B. it was where that
- C. where it was that
- D. where was it that
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**3、** There are three reading-rooms in our new library, _________about four hundred students in all.
- A. seating
- B. sit
- C. seated
- D. sitting
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**4、** It must have rained last light, ________ the ground is wet here and there.
- A. because
- B. as
- C. for
- D. since
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**5、** _________ no knowledge what may happen in one hundred years’ time.
- A. It is
- B. This is
- C. There is
- D. That’s
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**6、** They won’t go back to work unless their demands are _________.
- A. met
- B. permitted
- C. given
- D. replied
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**7、** ----___________? ----Go ahead.
- A. Can I use your pen for a while
- B. Shall I help you
- C. Will you start now
- D. Would you like some coffee
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**8、** What a poor memory I’ve got! I remember _________ the letter but forgot _________ it.
- A. to post … to stamp
- B. posting…to stamp
- C. to post…stamping
- D. posting…stamping
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**9、** __________ arriving in Tibet, the soldiers at once joined them __________ their work.
- A. At…with
- B. On…in
- C. For…with
- D. After…to
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**10、** ----Who told you about Dad’s illness? ----The doctor in ________.
- A. connection
- B. turn
- C. public
- D. charge
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**11、** Is __________ you want to say?
- A. all what
- B. which all
- C. that all
- D. all that
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**12、** From this hill top, you can see many islands ________ the coast.
- A. off
- B. on
- C. from
- D. along
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**13、** I’ve got to _________ up now. Someone is waiting to use the phone.
- A. give
- B. put
- C. look
- D. hang
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**14、** The river is so dirty that the water ___________.
- A. is not fit to drink
- B. doesn’t fit to drink
- C. is not fit to be drunk
- D. doesn’t fit for drinking
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**15、** ----Why are your group so happy? ----Our group _________ theirs in the oral English competition.
- A. has won
- B. have gained
- C. has beaten
- D. have defeated
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**16、** The furniture here looks more beautiful than _______ in our room
- A. one
- B. the one
- C. those
- D. that
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**17、** The area is _________ Wales.
- A. twice big as
- B. twice the size of
- C. twice the length of
- D. twice as long
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**18、** ----How ________ will he come here? ---- __________ two days.
- A. long; For
- B. often; Every
- C. soon; In
- D. fast; Within
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**19、** The clock _______ nine and we realized that it was already late.
- A. knocked
- B. struck
- C. beat
- D. hit
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**20、** ----How did you like the food here? ----Oh, excellent! We _______ a better hotel.
- A. couldn’t find
- B. won’t find
- C. mustn’t have found
- D. couldn’t have found
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**21、** I like _______ history; by studying it , I can gain ______ knowledge of the past events.
- A. /,a
- B. /,/
- C. the, a
- D. a, the
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**22、** ________ leaves the house last ought to turn off the lights.
- A. Anyone
- B. The person
- C. Whoever
- D. Who
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**23、** ----Is she going to school? ----No, _________.
- A. she doesn’t
- B. she goes by bus
- C. to the shops
- D. she is on the bike
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**24、** The roof of our lab was damaged by a _______ tree in the great storm last night.
- A. fall
- B. fallen
- C. falling
- D. felled
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**25、** Having made up his mind, he _________ to carry out his plan.
- A. set about
- B. set off
- C. set up
- D. set out
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**26、** We should invite Jenny to the party. ________ , she is fond of dancing and singing.
- A. First
- B. The first thing
- C. For one thing
- D. For another
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**27、** The girl seldom, if ________, turned to her brother for protection.
- A. never
- B. ever
- C. then
- D. always
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**28、** I won’t think _______ of him because of his shortcomings. He is a good boy after all.
- A. much
- B. little
- C. any less
- D. any more
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**29、** ----- You were tired after the sports meet, weren’t you? ----- Yes, ________ . I could hardly stand.
- A. not at all
- B. not a little
- C. not a bit
- D. not really
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**30、** He didn’t go into detail on the subject. He spoke __________.
- A. in common
- B. in particular
- C. in general
- D. in short
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**31、** Through the window I breathed fresh air _________ with the late night smell of grass.
- A. covered
- B. filled
- C. mixed
- D. taken
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**32、** Popular music is liked by many people, but it is not to everyone’s _________.
- A. hearing
- B. sound
- C. smell
- D. taste
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**33、** As a writer, he succeeded in calling _________ to many of the terrible wrongs that existed at that time.
- A. interest
- B. notice
- C. attention
- D. mind
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**34、** It was freezing cold. I put on my overcoat, but it didn’t seem to _________ .
- A. help
- B. heat
- C. make
- D. warm
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**35、** ------ Would you like to join us? ------ Sorry, I’m not _________ as any one of you.
- A. so a good player
- B. a player very good
- C. a so good player
- D. so good a player
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**36、** It is quite warm these days ________ November.
- A. at
- B. on
- C. with
- D. for
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**37、** The size of this kind of shoes is larger than ________ .
- A. my feet
- B. my shoes
- C. that of my feet
- D. that kind of shoes
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**38、** The car ________ on the way and we had to walk.
- A. broke down
- B. broke up
- C. broke out
- D. broke away
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**39、** They disagreed with one another and the meeting _________ without any result.
- A. broke down
- B. broke up
- C. broke out
- D. broke away
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**40、** Our school will _________ at the end of June and the students are ready to leave.
- A. break into
- B. break away
- C. break forth
- D. break up
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**41、** The colour TV set __________ that factory ___________ well.
- A. turned out by , sells
- B. producing by , is sold
- C. turning out from , sell
- D. produced from , is sold
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**42、** ------I’m afraid no one will agree with you. ------ I don’t think it _________ .
- A. is matter
- B. matters
- C. the matter
- D. minds
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**43、** -----I have two tickets for the film, will you go with me？ -----Yes, ___________ .
- A. it’s a pleasure
- B. with pleasure
- C. thanks
- D. I shall
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**44、** He studied very hard and in the end turned _________ .
- A. scientist
- B. a scientist
- C. the scientist
- D. out a scientist
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**45、** We would have our party in the hall _________ in the garden.
- A. instead
- B. in the place of
- C. rather than
- D. but not
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**46、** They move to another house to make _________ the new comers.
- A. a room for
- B. some room to
- C. room for
- D. rooms for
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**47、** I’ve visited a lot of different places and stayed in a lot of different hotels, but nothing else _________ this one.
- A. wins
- B. matches
- C. makes
- D. takes
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**48、** Poisons should be kept out of the __________ of children.
- A. reach
- B. catch
- C. grasp
- D. hold
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**49、** While playing in the garden, _________ .
- A. a stone hit me
- B. a stone fell down upon my shoulder
- C. my leg was heavily hit by a stone
- D. I was hit by a stone
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**50、** What he said at the meeting is _________ worth _________ .
- A. well, to listen
- B. very, listening
- C. very, to the listening to
- D. well, listening to
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**51、** What the young man said sounds strange, _________ it is true.
- A. but and
- B. yet and
- C. and but
- D. and yet
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**52、** -------David has made great progress recently. ------- __________ , and _________ .
- A. So he has …so you have
- B. So he has … so have you
- C. So has he … so have you
- D. So has he … so you have
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**53、** There was one listener in the lecture hall and only ten minutes later did I see ________ .
- A. the other
- B. another
- C. other one
- D. some other
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**54、** -------Would you do me a favor and give me a ride? ------- ___________ .
- A. Yes, that’s right
- B. No trouble at all
- C. Never mind
- D. With pleasure
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**55、** I shouldn’t have left Jimmy __________ I the room.
- A. single
- B. himself
- C. lonely
- D. alone
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**56、** The footsteps __________ his return.
- A. told
- B. expressed
- C. indicated
- D. announced
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**57、** __________ all her efforts, she failed to reach her aim.
- A. Together with
- B. In spite of
- C. Instead of
- D. Because of
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**58、** It won’t _________ a hero just to do one good deed.
- A. make
- B. be
- C. build
- D. bring
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**59、** After three days, our food _________ , and we had to return to our camp.
- A. gave away
- B. gave out
- C. gave off
- D. gave up
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**60、** --------Is __________ here? --------No, Bob and Tom have asked for leave.
- A. anybody
- B. somebody
- C. everybody
- D.nobody
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**61、** I’m sorry for what I’ve said. I __________ .
- A. hold it back
- B. take it back
- C. keep it back
- D. get it back
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**62、** The highway is about __________ .
- A. three thousand long miles
- B. long three thousand miles
- C. three-thousand-mile-long
- D. three thousand miles long
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**63、** ------- Can he carry the heavy bag alone? ------ ___________ . He is too old to do it.
- A. Yes, of course
- B. Afraid not
- C. Certainly
- D. I don’t think it
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**64、** A dozen years ________ , but the pretty woman remains __________ .
- A. passed, the same
- B. has gone by, unchanged
- C. passed by, unchanging
- D. went by, like before
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**65、** Huck _______ me staying there a little longer.
- A. remained
- B. kept on
- C. insisted on
- D. stuck to
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**66、** Anyone who has __________ the magazine please give it back immediately.
- A. taken
- B. received
- C. accepted
- D. brought
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**67、** Professor Smith lives _______ the second storey, this is _________ the second floor.
- A. in…on
- B. on…in
- C. in…in
- D. on…on
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**68、** The song sounds _________ , doesn’t it?
- A. well
- B. moved
- C. beautifully
- D. sweet
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**69、** _________ it is to swim in rivers or lakes.
- A. What a fun
- B. How fun
- C. What fun
- D. What funny
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**70、** Don’t get ________ to the dog. It is dangerous.
- A. closely
- B. nearly
- C. nearby
- D. close
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**71、** Every means __________ prevent the water from ___________ .
- A. are used to, being polluted
- B. was used to, polluting
- C. is used to, polluted
- D. is used to, being polluting

**72、** Let’s cut the apple __________ and share it.
- A. into half
- B. by half
- C. into halves
- D. in halves

**73、** He stole the money and was put __________ .
- A. at prison
- B. in prison
- C. at the prison
- D. in the prison
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**74、** He started early ___________ he could get there before nine.
- A. in order that
- B. because
- C. so as to
- D. in order to
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**75、** When you do fine, close work, such as reading or drawing, raise your eyes __________ and look into the distance.
- A. once for a while
- B. once in a while
- C. once at a while
- D. once of a while
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**76、** It was __________ that he couldn’t finish it alone.
- A. so difficult a work
- B. a so difficult job
- C. such a difficult work
- D. so difficult a job
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**77、** Everybody has to spend _________ in bed.
- A. some times
- B. some time
- C. sometime
- D. sometimes
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**78、** Never waste anything, but __________ never waste time.
- A. above all
- B. after all
- C. for all
- D. all above
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**79、** The boy stared at his book, ___________ .
- A. with pen in hand
- B. a pen in hand
- C. pen in his hand
- D. pen in hand
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**80、** She has _________ .
- A. a gift for languages
- B. a gift in languages
- C. a present for languages
- D. some cleverness to languages
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**81、** Look, __________ !
- A. here the bus comes
- B. here comes the bus
- C. here is the bus coming
- D. here the bus is coming
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**82、** He has _____________ .
- A. a ten-years-old daughter
- B. a ten-year-old daughter
- C. a ten year old daughter
- D. a ten-year-old-daughter
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**83、** She must have arrived, _________ she?
- A. mustn’t
- B. haven’t
- C. hasn’t
- D. didn’t
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**84、** No one enjoys __________ at.
- A. laughing
- B. to laugh
- C. being laughed
- D. to be laughed
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**85、** ------What do you suppose made her worried? ------ a gold ring.
- A. Lose
- B. loss
- C. losing
- D. Because of losing
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**86、** He has no interests, _________ his work.
- A. apart from
- B. beside
- C. besides
- D. accept
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**87、** Our teacher gives lessons in ___________ .
- A. compositions
- B. composition
- C. the composition
- D. a composition
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**88、** Yesterday we called at ________ .
- A. Mary’s sister’s
- B. my aunts
- C. a friend of my uncle’s
- D. my uncle
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. married
- B. married to
- C. been married to
- D. been married with
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**90、** His house is easily __________ from the rest. It has a large blue door.
- A. picked out
- B. picking out
- C. picked up
- D. picking
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**91、** It was about 600 years ago ________ the first clock with a face and an hour hand was made.
- A. that
- B. when
- C. before
- D. until

**92、** He has been here for __________ .
- A. two and a half month
- B. two and half a month
- C. two and the half months
- D. two and a half months

**93、** Shortly after the accident, two _______ police were sent to the spot to keep order.
- A. dozen of the
- B. dozens
- C. dozen of
- D. dozens of
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**94、** Your advice that she _________ more exercise is reasonable.
- A. takes
- B. take
- C. does
- D. carries out
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**95、** ___________ of the students in our class are from the north.
- A. Two nineth
- B. Two ninth
- C. Two ninths
- D. Two nineths
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**96、** I wasn’t _________ the decision until too late.
- A. informed
- B. formed
- C. informed of

**100、** The vitamins necessary for a healthy body are normally supplied by a ________ of fruit and green vegetables.

- B. using to live
- C. used to live
- D. to be used to living

- A. none of
- B. of which
- C. none
- D. none of which

- A. of last year’s
- B. those of last year’s

- A. into
- B. with
- C. to
- D. about
> ✅ 答案：**C**

### UNIT 2 (Section A)

**1、** The two passengers, as well as the driver, __________ in the traffic accident.
- A. were injured
- B. was wounded
- C. was harmed
- D. were damaged
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**2、** He stood waving until the train was out of .
- A. scene
- B. sight
- C. glimpse
- D. reach
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**3、** The desks and chairs can be to the height of each child.
- A. adjusted
- B. adopted
- C. measured
- D. objected
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**4、** I took the medicine but it didn’t have any .
- A. effort
- B. response
- C. effect
- D. relation
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**62、** It was not a serious accident, the car needs only some ______ repairs.
- A. major
- B. secondary
- C. minor
- D. primary
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- C. turn to
- D. turn against

- A. the air felt cool and fresh
- B. the sun had risen
- C. a boy was seen in a tree
- D. she saw a big bird in the tree
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**65、** Luckily, most ship _______ the flood last month.
- A. endured
- B. survived
- C. lived
- D. passed
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**66、** We can depend on John to make a _______ choice.
- A. sound
- B. loud
- C. noisy
- D. well
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**67、** It was getting late, so we set off _______ .
- A. to home
- B. at home
- C. for home
- D. home
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**68、** Finding it difficult to ______ to the climate there, he decided to move to the north.
- A. fit
- B. adopt
- C. suit
- D. adapt
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**69、** I suddenly _______ that it was past ten o’clock.
- A. realized
- B. recognized
- C. understand
- D. knew
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**70、** Good ________ ! I hope you’ll win the race.
- A. fortune
- B. luck
- C. chance
- D. fate
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**71、** ________ he works hard, I don’t mind when he will finish the experiment.
- A. As soon as
- B. As well as
- C. So far as
- D. So long as
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**72、** Please ________ the light before you go to sleep.
- A. die out
- B. go out
- C. turn off
- D. put on
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**73、** I tried to invite him to have a drink with me but he said that he was in such a hurry that he had no time to _______ .
- A. spend

- C. share
- D. spare
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. very, much more
- B. far, many more

- D. neglected
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**78、** All substances, ________ they are gaseous, liquid or solid, are made of atoms.
- A. though
- B. whether

- D. as
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. couple
- B. twice

> ✅ 答案：**C**

- A. as he is

> ✅ 答案：**C**

**81、** _______ that your son is well again, you no longer have anything to worry about.
- A. Since
- B. Now
- C. When
- D. After
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**82、** Most people will find it easier to speed on a highway when everybody _______ is driving over the speed limit.
- A. too
- B. also
- C. else
- D. alone
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**83、** You are ______ your time trying to persuade him. He will never join us.
- A. spending
- B. wasting
- C. losing
- D. missing
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**84、** Animals that could not _______ themselves to the changed environment perished and those that could survived.
- A. apply
- B. modify
- C. change
- D. adapt
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**85、** The tomato juice left brown _______ on my coat.
- A. trace
- B. spot
- C. dirt
- D. point
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**86、** Remember, nothing hurts concentration ________ reading too slowly.
- A. rather than
- B. as well as
- C. more than
- D. instead of
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**87、** Educational reforms in the 1980s _______ compulsory education to nine years.
- A. expanded
- B. stretched
- C. widened
- D. extended
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**88、** I don’t think John and I have anything ________ .
- A. in depth
- B. in common
- C. at random
- D. in detail
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**89、** A sense of well-being should always be an important part of life ______ your age.
- A. whatsoever
- B. whatever
- C. however
- D. no matter
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. circumstances
- B. occasion
- C. phenomenon
- D. status
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. live
- B. alive
- C. living
- D. lively
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**92、** Please keep me _______ your whereabouts in case I have something urgent to tell you.
- A. stuck to
- B. informed off
- C. linked with
- D. separated from
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. exerts
- B. presses
- C. provides
- D. poses
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**94、** I was not given any legal aid and I felt there was no justice in the country _______ .
- A. above all
- B. after all
- C. all in all
- D. in all
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**95、** A complete investigation into the causes of the accident should lead to improved standards and should _______ new operating procedures.
- A. result in
- B. subject to
- C. proceed with
- D. match with
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**96、** The students are _______ young people between the ages of sixteen and twenty.
- A. mostly
- B. most
- C. almost
- D. at most
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**97、** I took the children to the zoo today to ________ the party they missed yesterday.
- A. make up for
- B. take up for
- C. stand up for
- D. send up for
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**98、** The food smells very good, but what does it ________ like?
- A. taste
- B. feel
- C. try
- D. eat
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**99、** At that time they were poor, and they _______ a difficult time.
- A. went down
- B. went through

> ✅ 答案：**B**

### UNIT 7

**1、** The twins look so much alike that we can hardly _______ one from the other.
- A. separate
- B. contrast
- C. distinguish
- D. compare
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**2、** Most of the cattle are ________ under the trees.
- A. lying
- B. laying
- C. remaining
- D. learning
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**3、** Please________ my shirt, it’s torn.
- A. repair
- B. mend
- C. wash
- D. iron
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**4、** He is sometimes bad-tempered but he is a good fellow________.
- A. in heart
- B. with heart
- C. at heart
- D. by heart
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**5、** Many children chose “ Animal World” as their _______ TV program.
- A. preferred
- B. preferable
- C. favorable
- D. favorite
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**6、** Features such as height, weight, and skin color _______ from individual to individual and from face to face.
- A. change
- B. vary
- C. alter
- D. convert
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**7、** I caught a ______ of the taxi before it disappeared around the corner of the street.
- A. vision
- B. glimpse
- C. look
- D. scene
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**8、** You must walk through the underground _______ to get across that busy street.
- A. pass
- B. past
- C. passage
- D. passenger
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**9、** Miss Green didn’t care for the job offered by the shop-keeper; what she wanted was a post with more______.
- A. challenge
- B. struggle
- C. function
- D. fund
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**10、** You see the lightning _____ it happens, but you hear the thunder later.
- A. the instant
- B. for an instant
- C. on the instant
- D. in an instant
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**11、** I’ve made an ______ for you to see the dentist at 5 o’clock tomorrow.
- A. interview
- B. opportunity
- C. appointment
- D. occasion
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**12、** However, at times this balance in nature is _______, resulting in a number of possibly unforeseen effects.
- A. troubled
- B. disturbed
- C. confused
- D. puzzled
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**13、** The policeman went from door to door, _______ whether anyone had seen the lost boy.
- A. requiring
- B. acquiring
- C. inquiring
- D. informing
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**14、** Floods cause billions of dollars worth of property damage_______.
- A. relatively
- B. actually
- C. annually
- D. comparatively
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**15、** He made it his ______ to fetch water for the old woman.
- A. matter
- B. affair
- C. business
- D. operation
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**16、** It is our _______ policy t6hat we will achieve unity through peaceful means.
- A. consistent
- B. continuous
- C. considerate
- D. continual
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. optimistic

- C. outstanding
- D. obvious
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. mild

- C. light
- D. tender
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**19、** It is not the consciousness of man that determines his existence, but, ________ , his social existence determines his consciousness.
- A. on the average
- B. on the contrary
- C. on the whole
- D. on the other hand
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**20、** We can’t tell what home of the future will be like because new material and inventions are ______ being developed.
- A. commonly
- B. normally

- D. comparatively

- A. attention

- D. arrangement

**23、** As we can no longer wait for the delivery of our order, we have to _______ it.
- A. postpone

> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. order
- B. form
- C. state
- D. circumstance

- A. disapproval
- B. distaste
- C. dissatisfaction
- D. dismay

- B. proportion

- D. calculation

**100、** Although he has become rich, he is still very ________ of his money.

- D. careful

- D. dominate
> ✅ 答案：**C**

### UNIT 10

**1、** She did her best to stay awake, but the performance was so boring that she just ________ to sleep.
- A. dropped in
- B. dropped out
- C. dropped off
- D. dropped down
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**2、** Paul was a very lively child, always in ________.
- A. excellent spirits
- B. good spirits
- C. high spirits
- D. bright spirits
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**3、** Lilly made no attempt to ________ the problem.
- A. tackle
- B. try
- C. undertake
- D. achieve
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**4、** The boy began to cry and tears slowly ________ down his face.
- A. oozed
- B. trickled
- C. dribbled
- D. leaked
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**5、** On January 1', many people make a New Year ________.
- A. intention
- B. determination
- C. dedication
- D. resolution
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**6、** In this country it is against the ________ not to wear seat belts in a car.
- A. rule
- B. regulation
- C. law
- D. order
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**7、** Johnny's parents always let him have his own ________.
- A. will
- B. way
- C. wish
- D. demand
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**8、** Our telephone has been ________ for a month.
- A. out of order
- B. out of line
- C. out of touch
- D. out of place
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**9、** She was extremely ________, always ready to agree with other people's wishes.
- A. mellow
- B. moderate
- C. adaptable
- D. supple
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**10、** The woman tripped over the uneven pavement and ________ her elbow.
- A. distorted
- B. dislodged
- C. disabled
- D. dislocated
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**11、** He knew that he couldn't change anything so he just ________ himself to the situation.
- A. assigned
- B. resigned
- C. retired
- D. kept
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**12、** Game pie was a(n) ________ of this famous restaurant.
- A. singularity
- B. particularity
- C. speciality
- D. originality
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**13、** Mr. Williamson was acting peculiarly and seemed in a funny ________.
- A. state of affairs
- B. state of mind
- C. state of grace
- D. state of nature
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**14、** The scientists became very excited as they felt they were ________ of a discovery.
- A. on the crest
- B. on the surface
- C. on the borderline
- D. on the brink
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**15、** An ________ degree was conferred on the distinguished economist.
- A. honour
- B. honoured
- C. honorary
- D. honorable
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**16、** Mary is very set in her ways, but her brother has a more ________ attitude to life.
- A. changeable
- B. changing
- C. variable
- D. flexible
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**17、** My passport ________ last month, so I will have to get a new one.
- A. expired
- B. ended
- C. finished
- D. terminated
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**18、** Your room must be ________ by 12 o'clock, but your luggage may be left with the porter.
- A. evacuated
- B. vacated
- C. deserted
- D. abandoned
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**19、** My grandma's condition looks very serious and it is doubtful if she will ________.
- A. pull up
- B. pull in
- C. pull out
- D. pull through
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**20、** The purpose of the survey was to ________ the school inspectors with local school conditions.
- A. inform
- B. acquaint
- C. instruct
- D. notify
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**21、** Sam is being ________ for his stomachache at the hospital now.
- A. cured
- B. healed
- C. treated
- D. operated
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**22、** Because of his poor health, it took him a long time to ________ his bad cough.
- A. throw off
- B. throw away
- C. throw down
- D. throw over

**23、** People under 21 are not ________ to join the sports club.
- A. desirable
- B. eligible
- C. advisable
- D. admissible
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. repaired
- B. renewed
- C. restored
- D. renovated

**25、** I cannot bear the noise coming from my neighbour's house; it ________ me from my work.

- D. disturbs

- D. get by

- C. drawing
- C. inhaling
- D. puffing

- D. contrary
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- D. concerns
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. on

> ✅ 答案：**A**

**42、** The speaker had no ________ in himself when facing a large audience.

- D. confidence
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. projected
- B. revealed

- D. brought
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**44、** The football club tried to ________ the famous player with a generous offer of money.
- A. promote
- B. defend
- C. tempt
- D. force
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**45、** Cindy ________ some sweets from her bag and insisted they be shared among her friends.
- A. distributed
- B. pulled
- C. produced
- D. drew
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**46、** Not a ________ has been found so far that can help the police find the robber.

- B. clue
- C. mark
- D. sign
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**47、** She is a teacher ________ of our great respect.
- A. aware
- B. confident
- C. capable
- D. worthy
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**48、** They had ________ it for granted that they would succeed in their research.
- A. taken
- B. made
- C. had
- D. thought
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**49、** The final cost was considerably higher than the builder's original ________.

- B. sum
- C. account
- D. estimate
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**50、** He couldn't tell ________ from a distance whether it was Sam or his brother.

**51、** No matter how much progress we've made in our work, we shouldn't get ________.

- B. fashionable
- C. difficult
- D. intricate
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**53、** We should ________ all possible ways to create jobs for the unemployed.

- B. review
- C. explore
- D. make
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**54、** The coupon ________ you to a free meal in our new restaurant.

- C. grants
- D. offers
> ✅ 答案：**B**

> ✅ 答案：**D**

**56、** At the weekend, all the local TV channels provide extensive ________ of sporting events.

> ✅ 答案：**B**

**57、** In order not to be heard, the woman ________ that someone was moving about in the bush.

- B. shouted
- C. spoke
- D. whispered
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**58、** The appeal to the senses known as ________ is a common technique in poetry.
- A. imaginative

- C. ingenious

> ✅ 答案：**D**

**59、** The possibility that the explosion was caused by sabotage cannot be ________.

**60、** The headquarters of this oil company is in London while its ________ companies are located in different parts of the world.
- A. parent

- D. ultimate

**61、** Sitting on the table, two small cats were ________ round the inside of the empty ham tin.
- A. swallowing
- B. licking
- C. chewing
- D. munching

- A. moment
- B. dynasty
- C. epoch
- D. instant
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**63、** Absorbed in her work, she was totally ________ her surroundings.
- A. liable for
- B. oblivious of
- C. separated by
- D. concerned about
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**64、** The young man has ________ some brilliant scheme to double his income.
- A. come out

- C. come about
- D. come up with
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**65、** Social ________ vary greatly from country to country.
- A. customs
- B. rules
- C. habits
- D. regulations
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**66、** He is our best player, and is ________ to our team.
- A. internal
- B. integral
- C. intelligent
- D. insistent
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**67、** The Olympic Games ________ in 776 B. C. in Olympia, a small town in Greece.
- A. descend
- B. stemmed
- C. derived
- D. originated
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**68、** As a developing country, we must keep ________ with the rapid development of the world economy.
- A. motion
- B. pace
- C. speed
- D. step
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**69、** I gave the car a thorough ________ before buying it.
- A. inspection
- B. review
- C. survey
- D. check-up
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**70、** Sue joined the drama society but didn't seem to ________, so she left.
- A. fit out
- B. fit up
- C. fit in
- D. fit in with
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**71、** The company ________ the crisis by obtaining a low-interest loan from the bank.
- A. got round
- B. got through
- C. got off to
- D. got into
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**72、** The lecture was boring and the students could hardly ________ their yawns.
- A. depress
- B. suppress
- C. oppress
- D. press
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. out
- B. away
- C. in
- D. of
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. make do

- D. compensate for

- A. balance

**77、** The judge ruled that the evidence was inadmissible on the grounds that it was ________ to the issue at hand.
- A. irrational

- D. invalid

**78、** The mayor is a woman with great ________ and therefore deserves our political and financial support.
- A. intention

- C. instinct
- D. intensity
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**79、** When the working committee ________ to details, the proposed plan seemed unworkable.
- A. got down

- C. went off

**80、** He spoke so ________ that even his opponents were won over by his arguments.
- A. frankly
- B. emphatically
- C. determinedly
- D. convincingly
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**81、** Firms that use computers have found that the number of staff ________ is needed for quality control can be substantially reduced.
- A. whose
- B. as
- C. what
- D. that
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**82、** Many people are ________ to insect bites, and some even have to go to hospital.
- A. insensitive
- B. allergic
- C. sensible
- D. infected
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**83、** When you're driving on a motorway, you must obey the signs telling you to get into the right ________.
- A. way
- B. track
- C. road
- D. lane

**84、** The motorist had to ________ to avoid knocking the old woman down in the middle of the road.
- A. swerve
- B. twist
- C. depart
- D. swing
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**85、** In winter, drivers have trouble stopping their cars from ________ on icy roads.
- A. skating
- B. skidding
- C. sliding
- D. slipping
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**86、** This project would ________ a huge increase in defense spending.
- A. result
- B. assure
- C. entail
- D. accomplish
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**87、** The chances of a repetition of these unfortunate events are ________ indeed.
- A. distant
- B. slim
- C. unlikely
- D. narrow
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. separation
- B. division
- C. distinction
- D. difference
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**89、** In the present economic ________ we can make even greater progress than previously.
- A. air
- B. mood
- C. area
- D. climate
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**90、** Rite of Passage is a good novel by any standards; ________, it should rank high on any list of science fiction.
- A. consistently
- B. consequently
- C. invariably
- D. fortunately
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**91、** The diversity of tropical plants in the region represents a seemingly ________ source of raw materials, of which only a few have been utilized.
- A. exploited
- B. controversial
- C. inexhaustible
- D. remarkable
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**92、** While he was in Beijing, he spent all his time ________ some important museums and buildings.
- A. visiting
- B. traveling
- C. watching
- D. touring
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**93、** You must let me have the annual report without ________ by ten o'clock tomorrow morning.
- A. failure
- B. hesitation
- C. trouble
- D. fail
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**94、** As the director can't come to the reception, I'm representing the company ________.
- A. on his account
- B. on his behalf
- C. for his part
- D. in his interest
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**95、** Dreams are ________ in themselves, but, when combined with other data, they can tell us much about the dreamer.
- A. uninformative
- B. startling
- C. harmless
- D. uncontrollable
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**96、** During the famine, many people were ________ to going without food for days.
- A. sunk
- B. reduced
- C. forced
- D. declined
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**97、** The computer can be programmed to ________ a whole variety of tasks.
- A. assign
- B. tackle
- C. realize
- D. solve
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**98、** The team's efforts to score were ________ by the opposing goalkeeper.
- A. frustrated
- B. prevented
- C. discouraged
- D. accomplished
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**99、** I only know the man by ________ but I have never spoken to him.
- A. chance
- B. heart
- C. sight
- D. experience
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- B. distinction
- C. comparison

### UNIT 11

**1、** The plane found the spot and hovered close enough to ________ that it was a car.
- A. ensure
- B. examine
- C. verify
- D. testify
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**2、** The encouraging factor is that the ________ majority of people find the idea of change acceptable.
- A. numerous
- B. vast
- C. most
- D. massive
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**3、** The increase in student numbers ________ many problems for the universities.
- A. forces
- B. presses
- C. provides
- D. poses
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**4、** Please ________ from smoking until the aeroplane is airborne.
- A. refrain
- B. prevent
- C. resist
- D. restrain
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**5、** Reporters and photographers alike took great ________ at the rude way the actor behaved during the interview.
- A. annoyance
- B. offence
- C. resentment
- D. irritation
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**6、** Topics for composition should be ________ to the experiences and interests of the students.
- A. concerned
- B. dependent
- C. connecting
- D. relevant
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**7、** The novel contains some marvelously revealing ________ of rural life in the 19th century.
- A. glances
- B. glimpses
- C. glares
- D. gleams
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**8、** Sometimes the student may be asked to write about his ________ to a certain book or article that has some bearing on the subject being studied.
- A. reaction
- B. comment
- C. impression
- D. comprehension
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**9、** Picking flowers in the park is absolutely ________.
- A. avoided
- B. prohibited
- C. rejected
- D. repelled
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**10、** Tony has not the least ________ of giving up his research work.
- A. intention
- B. interest
- C. wish
- D. desire
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**11、** Two of the children have to sleep in one bed, but the other three have ________ ones.
- A. similar
- B. singular
- C. different
- D. separate
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**12、** Am I to understand that his new post ________ no responsibility with it at all?
- A. keeps
- B. supports
- C. carries
- D. possesses
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**13、** Animals that could not ________ themselves to the changed environment perished and those that could survived.
- A. change
- B. adapt
- C. modify
- D. conform
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**14、** The membership card entitled him ________ certain privileges in the club.
- A. on
- B. in
- C. at
- D. to
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**15、** Obviously, the chairman's remarks at the conference were ________ and not planned.
- A. substantial
- B. spontaneous
- C. simultaneous
- D. synthetic
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**16、** For the success of the project, the company should ________ the most of the opportunities at hand.
- A. obtain
- B. grasp
- C. catch
- D. make
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**17、** Failure to follow the club rules ________ him from the volleyball team.
- A. disfavored
- B. dispelled
- C. disqualified
- D. dismissed
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**18、** The discovery of new oil-fields in various parts of the country filled the government with ________ hope.
- A. eternal
- B. infinite
- C. ceaseless
- D. everlasting
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**19、** At first the company refused to purchase the equipment, but this decision was ________ revised.
- A. subsequently
- B. successively
- C. predominantly
- D. preliminarily
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**20、** The local police are authorized to ________ anyone's movements as they think fit.
- A. pause
- B. halt
- C. repel
- D. keep
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**21、** Have you ever received ________ of what has happened to her?
- A. the word
- B. words
- C. word
- D. the words
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**22、** Twelve is to three ________ four is to one.
- A. what
- B. as
- C. that
- D. like
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**23、** Things went well for her during her early life but in her middle age her ________ seemed to change.
- A. affair
- B. luck
- C. event
- D. chance
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**24、** Although I spoke to her about the matter several times, she took little ________ of what I said.
- A. remark
- B. warning
- C. notice
- D. attention
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**25、** The scheme was ________ when it was discovered it would be very costly.
- A. resigned
- B. surrendered
- C. released
- D. abandoned
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**26、** Yesterday my aunt bought some new ________ for her flat at the seaside.
- A. furniture
- B. furnitures
- C. possession
- D. possessions
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**27、** My cousin likes eating very much, but he isn't very ________ about the food he eats.
- A. special
- B. peculiar
- C. particular
- D. specific
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**28、** Your advice would be ________ valuable to him, who is now at a loss as to what to do first.
- A. exceedingly
- B. excessively
- C. extensively
- D. exclusively

**29、** More often than not, it is difficult to ________ the exact meaning of a Chinese idiom in English.
- A. exchange
- B. transfer
- C. convey
- D. convert
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**30、** She refused to ________ the door key to the landlady until she got back her deposit.
- A. hand in
- B. hand out
- C. hand down
- D. hand over
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**31、** The scientists have absolute freedom as to what research they think it best to ________.

- B. devote
- C. seek
- D. pursue
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**32、** The Olympic Games ________ in 776 B.C. in Olympia, a small town in Greece.
- A. originated
- B. stemmed
- C. derived
- D. descended
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. urgent
- B. instant
- C. prompt
- D. hasty
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**34、** The fact that the management is trying to reach agreement ________ five separate unions has led to long negotiations.
- A. over
- B. upon
- C. in
- D. with
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**35、** The chairman of the company said that new techniques had ________ improved their production efficiency.
- A. violently
- B. severely
- C. extremely
- D. radically
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**36、** The local authorities realized the need to make ________ for elderly people in their housing programmes.
- A. preparation
- B. requirement
- C. specification
- D. provision
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**37、** The guest team was beaten by the host team 2 ________ 4 in last year's CFA Cup Final.
- A. over
- B. in
- C. to
- D. against
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**38、** The police let him go, because they didn't find him guilty ________ the murder.
- A. of
- B. in
- C. over
- D. on
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**39、** The police have offered a large ________ for information leading to the robber's arrest.
- A. award
- B. compensation
- C. prize
- D. reward
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. only
- B. quite
- C. narrowly
- D. seldom
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- A. unjustified
- B. unjust
- C. misguided
- D. unaccepted
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. promising
- B. agreeing
- C. pleasing
- D. obliging
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. application
- B. bid
- C. proposal
- D. suggestion
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**44、** The party's reduced vote was ________ of lack of support for its policies.
- A. indicative
- B. positive
- C. revealing
- D. evident
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**45、** There has been a ________ lack of communication between the union and the management.
- A. regretful
- B. regrettable
- C. regretting
- D. regretted
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**46、** The teacher ________ expects his students to pass the university entrance examination.
- A. confidentially
- B. proudly
- C. assuredly
- D. confidently
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. normal
- B. average
- C. usual
- D. general
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. confesses
- B. declares
- C. claims
- D. confirms
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- A. pieces
- B. essays

- A. empty
- B. vacant
- C. free
- D. deserted
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**51、** The old couple will never ________ the loss of their son.
- A. get over
- B. get away
- C. get off
- D. get across
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. used

- C. tried
- D. practiced

**53、** Do you think there's any ________ of him passing the exam?

- B. opportunity
- C. occasion
- D. expectancy

- B. spending
- C. passing
- D. idling

**56、** On January 1st many people make a New Year ________.

- B. determination
- C. dedication

**57、** The two scholars worked at the task of writing a preface to the new dictionary for three hours ________ last night.

- D. in time

**60、** When the crowd saw the prize-fighter stretched out on the canvas, shouts and cheers ________ from it.

- D. broke upon

**61、** Outside my office window, there is a fire ________ on the right.

- A. valued

- D. worthy

- A. suit
- B. go

- B. pile
- C. helping

- C. view
- D. goal
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**69、** Fred has ________ kind of humour that can only be appreciated by those willing to search beneath the surface.
- A. an obvious
- B. a hidden

**70、** His argument does not ________ up to close scrutiny.
- A. hold
- B. stand
- C. come
- D. look
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**71、** The sight of the fruit salad made John's mouth ________.
- A. wet
- B. ooze
- C. water
- D. moisten
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**72、** He promised to return the book next day without ________.
- A. fail
- B. default
- C. trouble
- D. failure
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**73、** The ________ in David's character has hindered him from advancement in his career.
- A. weakness
- B. shortcoming
- C. demerit
- D. defect
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**74、** The government is doing a ________ of people's changing social habits.
- A. plan
- B. survey
- C. project
- D. research
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**75、** By cutting down trees we ________ the natural habitat of birds and animals.
- A. damage
- B. injure
- C. hurt
- D. harm
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**76、** The snow seems to have set ________ for the evening.
- A. upon
- B. in
- C. about
- D. down
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**77、** ________ of the department, I would like to thank Mr. Jones for his stimulating lecture.
- A. On behalf
- B. On account
- C. In person
- D. Instead
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**78、** Be careful with John; I think he has ________ motives for being so generous.
- A. ultimate
- B. ulterior
- C. interior
- D. hidden
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**79、** Paul earns very little as a classical cellist, ________ his brother's pop musicals have made millions.
- A. in contrast to
- B. compared with
- C. whereas
- D. though
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**80、** It is bad manners to ________ other people behind their backs.
- A. do up
- B. do by
- C. do away with
- D. do down
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**81、** Samuel has curly hair but his twin sister's is quite ________.
- A. even
- B. straight
- C. uneven
- D. regular
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**82、** My classmates stood there looking uneasily at me, puzzling why I hadn't got an­noyed this time when I couldn't get my own ________.
- A. way
- B. wish
- C. will
- D. desire
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**83、** The economists are not quite optimistic about the economic ________ for Europe.
- A. outlook
- B. lookout
- C. foresight
- D. project
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**84、** Jenny is not in the ________ for going to the party tonight.
- A. temper
- B. condition
- C. mood
- D. health
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**85、** There is no ________ in applying for that job, as you are not properly qualified.
- A. reason
- B. point
- C. result
- D. chance
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**86、** After leaving the art college, she ________ as an interior designer.
- A. took up
- B. got up
- C. set up
- D. put up
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**87、** Henry Adams felt so hungry that he ordered a double ________ of fish.
- A. part
- B. piece
- C. section
- D. portion
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**88、** It is hard to imagine a large city without policemen, but such was the ________ in London in the early 18th century.
- A. affair
- B. situation
- C. condition
- D. matter
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**89、** The Martian surface temperature is 100 degrees below zero at night, so that but-ter put outside will not ________.
- A. thaw
- B. dissolve
- C. melt
- D. fuse
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**90、** Having kissed her son, Eve gave him a dismissive ________ towards the car.
- A. push
- B. pull
- C. draw
- D. drag
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**91、** Wild ducks always fly in a definite ________.
- A. figure
- B. formula
- C. shape
- D. formation
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**92、** This road is completely ________ deep at night.
- A. isolated
- B. lonely
- C. deserted
- D. neglected
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**93、** It rained heavily last night; ________ the land was flooded.
- A. in other words
- B. consequently
- C. in addition
- D. besides
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**94、** When our university laboratory bought this microscope we were given a one year's ________.
- A. reservation
- B. security
- C. proof
- D. guarantee
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**95、** Don't ________ about lunch for the instructors, because they promised to get some on the way.
- A. bother
- B. fuss
- C. trouble
- D. think
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**96、** Caracus has been called the Los Angles of South America; at ________ they look exactly the same.
- A. short notice
- B. first sight
- C. first impression
- D. first appearance
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**97、** A Dictionary of the English Language , ________ by Dr Samuel Johnson, was the first real attempt as a systematic and interestingly written survey of English usage.
- A. constructed
- B. composed
- C. compiled
- D. collected
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**98、** After I heard that I took a deferred pass in botany, I was in a ________ emotional state.
- A. highly
- B. doubtfully
- C. greatly
- D. nervously
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**99、** Since I could not see anything through the microscope, ________ my careful adjustment, I gave up.
- A. for all
- B. above all
- C. after all
- D. in all
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**100、** When the Victorians had family reunions, the hosts went ________ their way to entertain the guests.
- A. in for
- B. over
- C. out of
- D. back on
> ✅ 答案：**C**

### UNIT 12

**1、** Peter ________ his breath in wonder when he saw the Christmas tree in the sitting room.
- A. kept
- B. held
- C. blew
- D. grasped
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**2、** The old buildings blend with the new ones in perfect ________, so a walk along Huai Hai Road is an enjoyable discovery.
- A. harmony
- B. order
- C. control
- D. action
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**3、** The energy gained from the sun can then be used during the night to enable the necessary chemical reactions to ________ in his body.
- A. conduct
- B. proceed
- C. progress
- D. practice
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**4、** When John was away on business, his neighbor gave his wife ________ with the housework.
- A. an aid
- B. a help
- C. a hand
- D. a lift
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**5、** We know she was always as ________ as her word, so we trusted her.
- A. good
- B. honest
- C. faithful
- D. true
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**6、** Newspapers vary greatly in their ________ to the government.
- A. opinion
- B. attitude
- C. bias
- D. comment
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**7、** The members in the testing team were quite ________ and could change their schedule upon request.
- A. lenient
- B. supple
- C. flexible
- D. gentle
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**8、** Marge's bedroom was in a ________, with books and papers covering every possible surface.
- A. litter
- B. mess
- C. rubbish
- D. disorder
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**9、** Tim, the footballer, always scores ________ at football matches, but he feels frustrated at each examination.
- A. points
- B. games
- C. goals
- D. targets
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**10、** After the cameraman had taken three or four photographs of the soldier ants, he found they had ________ all over his feet.
- A. gathered
- B. flocked
- C. crowded
- D. swarmed
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**11、** Sylvia was in low spirits because her throat infection left her very ________ and made her solo at the party impossible.
- A. dumb
- B. hoarse
- C. silent
- D. speechless
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**12、** It's not far to the village if we take the short ________ through the jungle.
- A. pass
- B. corner
- C. turning
- D. cut
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**12、** Working under the new expert who always encourages creativity in young peo­ple, we have been ________ with better ideas in test design.
- A. coming down
- B. coming out
- C. coming along
- D. coming up
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. drops
- B. measure
- C. dose
- D. limit
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**14、** She is as ________ as her mother is beautiful.
- A. plain
- B. common
- C. dull
- D. humble
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**15、** Get him to sign the contract before he has second ________.
- A. plans
- B. thoughts
- C. intentions
- D. ideas
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**16、** If you keep getting wrong numbers, your phone could be ________.
- A. deceptive
- B. ineffective
- C. deficient
- D. defective
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**17、** It was difficult to ________ a date which was convenient for everyone.
- A. decide
- B. arrange
- C. organize
- D. provide
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**18、** "Do you like your new job?" "Yes, it's very ________ work."
- A. enjoyable
- B. joyful
- C. enjoyed
- D. enjoying
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**19、** It is ________ whether Sam will come to attend his best friend's funeral as he has taken the town's money and made off with it.
- A. unlikely

- D. unexpected

**20、** To attend the grand dinner party, the French cabinet minister had a new suit made to ________.
- A. order
- B. direction

**21、** Present at the Christmas party were the two princesses and their ________ husbands and the Duke of Edinburgh.
- A. respectable
- B. respectful

- D. respected

**22、** In an attempt to get to the ________ of the problem, the reporter interviewed every eyewitness.
- A. root

**23、** The narrow, sunless hall smelled ________ of stale cabbage.
- A. uninterestingly

**24、** The ordinary Zulu fighters were not ________ to Shaka's plan of throwing away their sandals.
- A. opposed
- B. objected

- D. conflicting

**25、** The tourists ________ through the fog, trying to read what was engraved on the gravestone Shakespeare had chosen for himself.
- A. glanced

- D. peeped

- A. nothing

- D. not

- C. choice
- D. purpose

- A. in

- D. of
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. access

- D. admission

- A. situated

- D. concentrate

- A. song

- D. motto

- A. pick
- B. spot
- C. discover
- D. realize
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- A. deathly

- C. fatal
- D. mortal

- A. extension

- A. cuts

- D. orders

- A. harm
- B. sorrow
- C. shame
- D. shock
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**42、** I had to pay ________ on a carpet I brought in through the Customs today.
- A. taxes
- B. rates
- C. fines
- D. duty
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- D. shows

- D. unbelievable

- D. relate
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. when

- D. if
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- C. that
- D. then
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. put down

- D. put out

- A. very

- D. utterly

- A. how

- D. indicate
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. spoilt

> ✅ 答案：**A**

**98、** The National Gallery in London has more visitors than ________ any other because of its free admission.
- A. practically
- B. really
- C. actually
- D. completely
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**99、** Mr. Johnson had a terrible cold and could not stop ________.
- A. sneezing
- B. sighing
- C. yawning
- D. spitting
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**100、** We haven't the ________ idea why we got such a massive telephone bill last month.
- A. dampest
- B. mistiest
- C. foggiest
- D. rainiest
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**101、** David, who was spoilt, having been born with a ________ spoon in his mouth, was a very difficult student.
- A. diamond
- B. iron
- C. gold
- D. silver
> ✅ 答案：**D**

### UNIT 13

**1、** We keep our curriculum _______ the changes in the business world more, I think, than other business school.
- A. abreast of
- B. ahead of
- C. aloof from
- D. aside from
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**2、** Ronny’s steps died away, and there was a moment of ______ silence.
- A. absent
- B. abstract
- C. absolute
- D. accurate
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**3、** In the past, common people can not possibly get _______ to the Imperial Palace.
- A. approach
- B. access
- C. permission
- D. abdication
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**4、** Melting snow _______ for the regular spring floods in this areas.
- A. accumulates
- B. accounts
- C. competes
- D. deposits
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**5、** Very few foreigner travel in Sweden in winter _______ the intense cold.
- A. on account of
- B. thanks to
- C. since
- D. for the sake of
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**6、** The writer of the novel is well _______ with the problem in the company because she has been working there for almost a decade.
- A. enlightened
- B. informed
- C. acquainted
- D. acknowledged
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**7、** He was unable to ______ to the group what he meant.
- A. get in
- B. get across
- C. get along
- D. get off
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**8、** Individuals with characteristics ________ survive and make up an increasingly large proportion of their species, thus evolving new conditions.
- A. adapting to existing conditions
- B. adapting to exist conditions
- C. adapted to existing conditions
- D. adapted to existed conditions
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**9、** Family planning has been _______ by the Chinese government for several decades.
- A. advocated
- B. invoked
- C. adapted
- D. convoked
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**10、** The news had already been _______ before it was officially announced.
- A. on the air
- B. by air
- C. in the air
- D. among air
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**11、** ________ to the art exhibition.
- A. All but he and I am going
- B. All but him and me are going
- C. All but him and I am going
- D. All but he and me are going
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**12、** My dress was too large so I went to the tailor’s and have it ______ .
- A. adjusted
- B. changed
- C. altered
- D. turned
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**13、** His letter ________ an admission that he has done wrong.
- A. exposes to
- B. yields to
- C. amounts to
- D. ascribes to
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**14、** Before you begin, try to __________ the difficulties.
- A. expect
- B. anticipate
- C. participate
- D. precipitate
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**15、** The speaker ______ the feeling of his audience rather than to their reason.
- A. aroused to
- B. aroused for
- C. appealed to
- D. appealed for
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**16、** Today, housework has been made much easier by electrical _________ .
- A. facilities
- B. appliances
- C. instruments
- D. equipments
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**17、** Mrs. Harris’ ______ smile let us know that our speeches were well done.
- A. approving
- B. permitting
- C. admitting
- D. agreeing
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**18、** A good judge tries to be fair and does not make ________ decision.
- A. objective
- B. sympathetic
- C. definite
- D. arbitrary
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**19、** After the accident, she began to wear a set of ________ teeth.
- A. supernatural
- B. superficial
- C. artificial
- D. superstitious
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**20、** He must have been almost the same age _______ when I first met him.
- A. as was my father
- B. like my father was

- D. which my father was
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**21、** He _______ his belief that she was innocent.
- A. affirmed

- D. stressed
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**22、** I’d like it to be my ______ , for I can do it only in my spare time.
- A. vocation
- B. vacation

- D. aversion
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**23、** When American astronaut Glen returned to the earth a hearty welcome ______ him.
- A. waited for

- C. expected
- D. awaited
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**25、** Skyscrapers also interfere with television reception, ____ bird flyways, and obstruct air traffic.

**27、** Difficulties and hardships have _______ the best qualities of the young geologist.

**28、** In general, our society is becoming one of giant enterprises directed by ______ management.

**34、** Your _______ at the party would be deeply appreciated. If you could not come, please inform us at your earliest convenience.
- A. company

**35、** The actor became very ________ after he made a name for himself.

- C. haunted
- D. contemptuous

**36、** I don’t quite understand your attitude toward the government from your lecture. Could you ________ on that?

**37、** I’d get it for you _______ I could remember who last borrowed it.
- A. except that

**38、** What I am telling you is strictly ________ .Don’t let anyone know of it.

- A. contending

> ✅ 答案：**D**

- D. incapable

- A. refused
- B. denied
- C. opposed
- D. pretended
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- D. dead

> ✅ 答案：**B**

- B. specialized
- C. distinguished
- D. distinctive
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**73、** I _______ watching this performance because it is wonderful.
- A. delight
- B. enjoy
- C. amuse
- D. please
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**74、** The park ________ as far as the river.
- A. advances
- B. extends
- C. enlarges
- D. expands
> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. components
- B. factors
- C. elements
- D. solids
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- A. assessment

- D. evaluation

**77、** It is well-known that the retired workers in our country are _______ free medical care.
- A. entitled to
- B. associated with
- C. involved in
- D. assigned to
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**78、** My students found the book _______ . It provided them with an abundance of information on the subject.
- A. enlightening
- B. distracting
- C. confusing
- D. interesting
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**79、** Your advice would be ________ valuable to him, who is present at his wit’s end.
- A. exceedingly
- B. extensively
- C. excessively
- D. exclusively
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**80、** _______ all our kindness to help her, Sara refused to listen.
- A. At
- B. For
- C. In
- D. On
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**81、** The light gradually _______ and shapes and colors grew fainter.
- A. faded
- B. disappeared
- C. melted
- D. died
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**82、** I have a _______ belief in his honesty.
- A. determined
- B. certain
- C. firm
- D. powerful
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**83、** I can’t _______ what has happened to the vegetables, for they were freshly picked this morning.
- A. figure out
- B. look out
- C. draw out
- D. work out
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**84、** The statue would be perfect but for a few small _______ in its base.
- A. mistakes
- B. flaws
- C. weaknesses
- D. errors
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**85、** When the engine would not start, the mechanic inspected all the parts to find what was at ______ .
- A. wrong
- B. fault
- C. trouble
- D. difficulty
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**86、** The river is already _______ its banks because of excessive rainfall, and the city is threatened with a likely flood.
- A. parallel to
- B. flat on

- D. flush with
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**87、** China is trying to keep her population _________ down.
- A. birth
- B. size
- C. increase
- D. growth
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. go after

- C. go by
- D. go on
> ✅ 答案：**C**

- A. safety
- B. confirmation
- C. security
- D. conservation
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**90、** To survive in the intense trade competition, we must ________ market ______ demand.
- A. improve, to
- B. guarantee, for
- C. enhance, to
- D. gear, to
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**91、** When he writes, he always keeps a dictionary ________ .
- A. by hand
- B. in hand
- C. at hand
- D. out of hand
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**92、** We should always keep in mind that _______ decisions often lead to bitter regrets.
- A. urgent
- B. instant
- C. hasty
- D. prompt
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**93、** The work in the office was ______ by a constant stream of visitors.
- A. confused
- B. reversed
- C. perplexed
- D. hampered
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**94、** The wound is not yet _______ .
- A. treated
- B. healed
- C. remedied
- D. cured
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**95、** I am _______ the habit of brushing my teeth twice daily.
- A. on
- B. off
- C. in
- D. with
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**96、** Although the main characters in the novel are so true to life, they are entirely ________ .
- A. imaginary
- B. imagined
- C. imaginative
- D. imaginable
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- A. incompatible
- B. incredible
- C. indefinite
- D. indispensable

- A. consciousness
- B. instinct
- C. impulse

> ✅ 答案：**B**

**100、** The tragedy of the Challenger _______ an ongoing controversy on all aspects of America’s space program.
- A. arose
- B. resulted

## 第二部分 语法与结构

### 语法（一）

**1.** The workers are paid _______.
- A. by an hour
- B. by the hour
- C. by hours
- D. by hour
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**2.** ______ found four-leaf clover is considered a lucky sign.
- A. Rarely
- B. It’s rarely
- C. The rarely
- D. Despite its being rarely
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**3.** We _______ our breakfast when an old man came to the door.
- A. just have had
- B. just had
- C. have just had
- D. had just had
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**4.** This is the first time he _______ out with her alone.
- A. had been
- B. is
- C. has been
- D. was
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**5.** His performance in the exam is excellent. He _______ a lot of time studying.
- A. must spend
- B. must have spent
- C. may spend
- D. has spend
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**6.** _______ the salary meets my expectation, I will accept the job offered.
- A. Due to
- B. Even if
- C. Unless
- D. Provided that
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**7.** I wouldn’t be saying this _______ I were sure of the facts.
- A. thanks to
- B. unless
- C. provided that
- D. in case
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**8.** If law and order _______ not preserved, neither the citizen nor her property is safe.
- A. is
- B. was
- C. are
- D. were
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**9.** One of the students who _______ to the authority about the problem of pollution raises his hand.
- A. have written
- B. has written
- C. writes
- D. write.
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**10.** The linebacker and captain of the team _______ most valuable player.
- A. have been selected
- B. have selected
- C. has been selected
- D. has selected
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**11.** _______ of books on how to prepare for the entrance examination are compiled by him.
- A. The number
- B. The amount
- C. A number
- D. A great deal
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**12.** The majority _______ on Ben’s side. But Peter disagrees with them on two points.
- A. were
- B. was
- C. is
- D. are
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**13.** Whether or not the new plan will yield any positive result _______ to be seen.
- A. remain
- B. is remained
- C. remains
- D. have remained
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**14.** More than one best authority on science fiction _______ James Newman a science-fiction writer.
- A. does not consider
- B. do consider
- C. do not consider
- D. consider
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**15.** John’s father, along with his two uncles, _______ in New York one more day.
- A. demand that he stay
- B. demands that he stays
- C. demands that he stay
- D. demand that he stays
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**16.** The cat with her kittens _______ in the bay window when I entered the room yesterday.
- A. were sitting
- B. sat
- C. had sat
- D. was sitting
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**17.** The welfare department , as well as the other services, will have ______ budget cut.
- A. their
- B. its
- C. his
- D. our
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**18.** The criteria for promotion ______ clearly stated.
- A. has
- B. have
- C. is
- D. are
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**19.** It is said that most of the film we are going to see ______ the organized bank robbery.
- A. describes
- B. describe
- C. has described
- D. described
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**20.** The museum _______ the public every day.
- A. opens
- B. is being opened
- C. is opened
- D. is open to
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**21.** The jury _______ in opinion about “sentence to death.”
- A. disagrees
- B. disagree
- C. agrees
- D. has agreed
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**22.** There was nothing they could do _______.
- A. but wait
- B. except waiting
- C. only to wait
- D. unless they waited
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**23.** To her parents’ surprise, despite frequent absence, she managed ______ her studies.
- A. to be catching
- B. to catch up on

### 语法（二）

**1.** The fog _____ dense, all the ferry boats were suspended.
- A. is
- B. being
- C. having been
- D. was
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**2.** Doctor by training _____ he was, he proved himself to be a greater writer.
- A. though
- B. although
- C. since
- D. if
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**3.** She said she wouldn’t call us the next day, ____she?
- A. would
- B. wouldn’t
- C. did
- D. didn’t
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**4.** The prisoner pleaded that he _____ allowed to see his family, who did not yet know of his conviction.
- A. is
- B. was
- C. be
- D. could be
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**5.** A thorough overhauling of the accounts has revealed _____ a tax evader.
- A. him to be
- B. him being
- C. that he be
- D. that he had been
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**6.** The flowers need _____ . Would you like me to do it for you?
- A. watering
- B. to water
- C. water
- D. to be watering
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**7.** Western Nebraska generally receives less snow than _____ eastern Nebraska.
- A. does
- B. in
- C. it does in
- D. in it does
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**8.** Let us get out quickly. It is no good ____ time.
- A. waste
- B. to waste
- C. wasting
- D. to have wasted.
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**9.** No sooner had we finished the conversation _____ we heard a knock on the door.
- A. when
- B. than
- C. then
- D. until
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**10.** Because of the unexpected changes, they postponed _____ us an answer.
- A. to give
- B. have given
- C. to have given
- D. giving
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**11.** He didn’t allow ____ in his room, actually he did not allow his family ____ at all.
- A. to smoke----to smoke
- B. smoking---- to smoke
- C. to smoke----smoking
- D. smoking----smoking
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**12.** Can you have your report ____ tomorrow?
- A. finish
- B. to finish
- C. finished
- D. finishing
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**13.** From what is mentioned above we can draw a conclusion ____ electric energy can be turned into light energy.
- A. what
- B. which
- C. how
- D. that
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**14.** The judge required that each witness ____his name in ink.
- A. sign
- B. will sign
- C. signs
- D. signed
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**15.** She was trying ____ by the teacher in class.
- A. avoiding questioning
- B. to avoid being questioned
- C. avoiding to question
- D. to avoid questioning
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**16.** The winter vacation ____ over, he got down to his work again.
- A. was
- B. were
- C. had been
- D. being
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. a smoking
- B. smoke
- C. to smoke
- D. smoking
> ✅ 答案：**D**

- A. no more
- B. not more
- C. any more
- D. much more

- A. There is deafness
- B. It is deafness

- D. ought to have dropped
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**350.** I usually walk _______ the river bank over there, but today I feel tired and won’t walk _______ far.
- A. as long as…so
- B. as far as…that
- C. as often as…such
- D. as soon as…very
> ✅ 答案：**B**

## 第三部分 单选练习

### Exercise 1

**1.** Literature must consist of words, ________ music must consist of sounds.
- A. even though
- B. so that
- C. just as
- D. ever since

**2.** When workers are organized in trade unions, employers find it hard to lay them ________.
- A. off
- B. aside
- C. out
- D. down
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**3.** Their different attitudes to the difficulties ________ different results.
- A. arrived at
- B. led to
- C. caused by
- D. turned out
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**4.** If you travel through the villages in the small local buses, you will swiftly come into close ________ with the local people.
- A. carriage
- B. contact
- C. choice
- D. circuit
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**5.** In ________ of speed, safety and price, the high-speed railway system is perhaps the world’s model railway.
- A. terms
- B. case
- C. need
- D. search
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**6.** Applicants for the Olympic volunteers must be able to ________ excellent services to meet the demands of the Games.
- A. apply
- B. make
- C. contribute
- D. use
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**7.** It is reported that the floods have left about ________ people homeless.
- A. two thousand
- B. two-thousands
- C. two thousands
- D. two thousands of
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**8.** Many young couples do not take marriage ________ and they get married and divorced easily.
- A. eagerly
- B. highly
- C. seriously
- D. severely
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**9.** Many people believe that ________ one has, ________ one is, but actually it is not true.
- A. the more money; the happier
- B. the more money; the more happy
- C. the less money; the happier
- D. the less money; the more happy
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**10.** Mr. Wang is getting too old for football. He decides to ________ tennis instead.
- A. take in
- B. take up
- C. take for
- D. take over
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**11.** It is not always reliable to make a conclusion on the ________ of the first impression.
- A. basic
- B. base
- C. basement
- D. basis
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**12.** She tries her best to ________ the changes in fashion.
- A. keep up with
- B. put off
- C. come up with
- D. go by
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**13.** The road to the island only appears when the tide has ________.
- A. sprinkled
- B. receded
- C. tilted
- D. diminished
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**14.** The improvements in technology have ______ the prices of computer considerably in recent months.
- A. brought back
- B. brought down
- C. brought on
- D. brought up
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**15.** As the manager’s private secretary, Helen has easy ______ to all his correspondence.
- A. access
- B. approach
- C. accent
- D. response
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**16.** The editor asked his journalists not to ______ any details in their reports of the accident.
- A. leave for
- B. leave on
- C. leave off
- D. leave out
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**17.** Had I had a little more money on me, I could have bought ______ more postcards.
- A. few
- B. a little
- C. little
- D. a few
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**18.** Oh no! We have no gas. Robert ______ to fill the tank.
- A. could have forgotten
- B. should have forgotten
- C. must have forgotten
- D. might have forgotten
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**19.** We can see from the map that Japan lies ______ the east of China, ______ the east of Asia and faces the Pacific ______ the east.
- A. in; on; to
- B. to; in; on
- C. on; to; on
- D. to; on; to
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**20.** The “Chinese Dream” is ______ dream to improve people’s well-being and ______ dream of harmony, peace and development.
- A. the; a
- B. a; a
- C. a; the
- D. the; the
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**21.** You have to do those exercises ______ you want to perform poorly in the exam tomorrow.
- A. if
- B. as
- C. unless
- D. provided
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**22.** It’s too late to go out. ______, it’s going to rain.
- A. Otherwise

- C. However
- D. Therefore
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**23.** Many students thought about the third question for quite a while and had great difficulty answering it. Obviously, it was ______ all.
- A. more confusing than
- B. as confusing as
- C. the most confused of
- D. the most confusing of
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**24.** You don’t have the right to blame me ______ being late, for I was busy typing your business invitations.
- A. with
- B. of
- C. for
- D. on
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**25.** There is a warning at the bottom of the form that applicants are requested to hand in their forms ______ person.

- D. in
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**26.** I followed her ______ but I still couldn’t work out how to use the sewing machine.

- D. comments
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**27.** As National Day is round the corner, Shanghai is ______ a festive atmosphere.

- D. surrounded with 包围（实体）
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**28.** As each black creature ______ and flapped away into the graying sky, she watched it, tears in her eyes.
- A. fluttered 振翅

- C. fussed
- D. ferried

**29.** The couple have tried hard to save their troubled marriage ______ their children.
- A. in contrast to

- D. regardless of

**30.** There seemed to be no ______ to their financial problem.
- A. method

- D. solution
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**31.** There was nothing we could do ________ wait at that moment.
- A. but

> ✅ 答案：**A**

**32.** Some companies have introduced flexible working time with less emphasis on pressure ________.

> ✅ 答案：**A**

**33.** Steven ________ my letter; otherwise he would have replied before now.
- A. has received

- D. ought to have received
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**34.** We must ________ that the experiment is controlled as rigidly as possible.
- A. assure

- C. ensure
- D. issue
> ✅ 答案：**C**

**35.** I need to move to a larger apartment. Do you know of any ________ one in this neighborhood?

- D. empty
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**36.** Religion has a great ________ on man's thought.

- D. influence
> ✅ 答案：**D**

**37.** The children are getting more and more excited when Christmas is ________ near.
- A. drawing
- B. joining
- C. taking
- D. operating
> ✅ 答案：**A**

**38.** Many companies provide their employees ________ free lunch during the weekdays.
- A. by

> ✅ 答案：**B**

**39.** The work seemed easy at first, but it ________ to be quite difficult.

> ✅ 答案：**B**

- A. waken

> ✅ 答案：**C**

**41.** She pretended ________ indifferent but her facial expression betrayed her.
- A. to be
- B. being
- C. be
- D. been
> ✅ 答案：**A**

- D. two mile's

**43.** Although Rosemary had suffered from a serious illness for years, she lost ________ of her enthusiasm for life.
- A. some

> ✅ 答案：**C**

**44.** Frank used to be ________ strong. But now he's down to his last tooth.

- C. find
- D. keep
> ✅ 答案：**B**

**8.** He likes novels, _______ I like poetry.

- D. so

**26.** Having finished his homework, he went on ________ a novel written by Dickens.

- D. would happen

- D. worked

**29.** I think it was high time they ________ to take you more seriously.

- D. begun

**30.** We should learn from those ________ are always ready to help others.

**31.** All I can do now is nothing ________.

**32.** But for the favorable weather, we ________ in such a good harvest.

**35.** There seems to be no choice but ________ the offer.

**36.** By the time your plane lands tonight, I ________ at the airport for 3 hours.

**37.** The activity was delayed, ________ was exactly ________ we wanted.

**38.** So little ________ with each other that the neighbors couldn't settle their difference.

**39.** It was in 2017 ________ Donald Trump became president of the United States.

**41.** I tore open the box only ________ that some papers were missing.

**42.** Either my parents or my elder brother ________ going to water the garden.

**46.** If you had told me earlier, I ______ to meet you at the hotel.
